# Kedra worklog

Shared continuation record. Historical entries below are preserved; paths they name can refer to earlier revisions.

## Current project status

Last updated: 2026-10-05 (Europe/Kyiv).

- **Delivery:** five native-stack drafts remain published (PR33–37), none merged. D1/D3/D4 retain their local qualification; D5 independent signed-cache rebuild now passes. Full D2/D5 acceptance remains incomplete.
- **Hosted boot:** exact D2 source332f450/run37217459554 and D5 source8c35755/run37217459773 pass full candidate/disk/UEFI Secure Boot, lockdown, enforcing SELinux and exact guest-image checks. The containerd and typed inspection-default fixes are verified. These are unsigned disposable boot results, not installation or production publication.
- **Direct updates:** all four QMP-fixed hosted ARM runs now complete the insecure-boot refusal and QMP shutdown in368–411 seconds. They subsequently fail the fresh-install phase at its7200-second deadline. Bounded diagnostics show firmware/GRUB/kernel/systemd startup but no installation-complete marker; the later guest boundary remains unknown. Cleanup passes and all paired x86 jobs pass. Protected main-only signing remains unrun.
- **Local D2:** producer1dc/fixture6146d96 completes public refusal, fresh installation, signed A/B/rollback and finish with actual new waits0, security/native-artifact/data checks and independently verified cleanup. The original Docker execution exits0; its lost host-parent wait stays unavailable. TC05 exact candidatee0fd passes13/13. TC03 genuine current foundation/catalog/composer on frozen580/material2271 passes exact-image jq/SQLite, separate library loading, nine refusals and final verification. The original resource-stop remains failed; its separately admitted retry passes and settles within its60-minute bound.
- **D5 completed cases:** exact-source external signed transfer/run/reuse/forced support+report rebuild/rerun43 pass. Nine metadata refusals, valid-from/final-valid-second admission, staging expiry, incomplete closure and corrupt existing content refusals pass. Eight genuine-lease refusals/restored recovery and root-publication ENOSPC recovery/retry pass. Prior source-import ENOSPC and stopped-reader cases retain their passes. See WL-20261004-37.
- **D5 remaining:** ordinary C2/C3, actual owner/filesystem C5 refusals, cleanup-failure/orphan recovery and source-import journal/root/snapshot-copy ENOSPC recovery/retry pass in their recorded scopes. Killed-process cleanup and native transaction/binding failure cases remain unqualified. Native C6 still has one captured pre-ID window but no completed retry/window after earlier automatic review; pending native state is preserved. Empty root temporary cleanup is not promised by the observed recovery result.
- **Retained OS:** signed main sourceb224d571/ARM7a8f6324 and recovery image9d6eb030/ISOcb8578a4 remain historical baselines. No workstation installation, enrollment or reboot is authorized here.
- **Ownership / next:** primary Codex owns tracked source/Git/status and is qualifying a bounded D2 installer-stage diagnostic change. Astra reviewers inspect source and retained evidence without launching local workloads. A separate medium-scope niri/home-artifact follow-up is being planned only; no home implementation is authorized. Preserve protected resources/every failed attempt and the blocked native boundary.
- **Latest source gates:** exact published heade908bb2 passes both architectures in push Check37241140437 and PR Check37241143014. Final ARM failure artifacts from runs37234495769/37234495358/37234495136/37234496224 are inspected; their installation failures remain distinct from these standard source-gate passes.

### WL-20260925-03 — 2026-09-25 — Pass environment secrets to the reusable signer
- Agent / state: Claude Code (Opus 5.5); completed.
- Scope / base: `codex/release-env-secrets` from main `543acfd` (PR #16 merge); release pipeline, both targets.
- Completed: The first per-target push release [36190624411](https://github.com/Reidond/kedra/actions/runs/36190624411) built both targets (desktop candidate `sha256:9df9cf2a…`, utm build pushed) but both sign-image jobs failed closed with `initializing private key: invalid pem block`: the environment variables (public key, fingerprint) resolved while both signing secrets were empty. A disposable probe (temporary `kedra-probe` environment with a dummy secret and branch, both deleted) showed in run [36191986665](https://github.com/Reidond/kedra/actions/runs/36191986665) that a called job declaring `environment:` sees the environment secret as empty without `secrets: inherit` and present with it. `release.yml` now inherits secrets for both callers; the repository has no repository-level secrets, so only each signer job's own environment secrets resolve. The log also exposed an ineffective guard carried over from the original workflow: `test -n "$KEY" && test -n "$PASS"` does not stop a `bash -e` step when the left side fails; it is split into two commands. Docs and the kedra-github-actions skill record the observed behaviour. No image was signed; stable tags did not move; unsigned `-builds` tags from that run remain inert.
- Checks / evidence: pass — probe run 36191986665 (`plain` SECRET_EMPTY, `inherit` SECRET_PRESENT); actionlint on both release workflows. Not-run — a signing run with this fix (release runs only on main).
- Remaining / blockers: the first utm publication and the desktop republish wait for this merge.
- Next: merge, watch the push release for both signed publications.
- Outcome: PR #17 merged as `bfd42d1`; its checks (workspace both architectures, direct GHCR VM) passed. Push release [36193673337](https://github.com/Reidond/kedra/actions/runs/36193673337) passed every job for both targets. Anonymous readback: `ghcr.io/reidond/kedra-desktop:stable` = `sha256:6ae1c07cf2e0b585da5775eb4abd555923c71b462dc8fd838fc6dc9ee0debfe6` (amd64, desktop/x86_64, run 28/1, `bfd42d1`, Fedora 44.20260925.0) and the first `ghcr.io/reidond/kedra-utm:stable` = `sha256:b9a5c32e92b27ecc93e1a6f158befeeef3b446b8ca6c71754b9b04e7e7d46bef` (arm64, utm/aarch64, run 28/1, `bfd42d1`, 44.20260925.0); both `sha256-<digest>.sig` tags return 200.

- Agent / state: Claude Code (Opus 5.5) with the owner operating the UTM window; in-progress.

  - Local media: `installer/utm/kedra-utm.py iso` built `kedra-utm-44-b9a5c32e92b27ecc.iso` (3,171,155,968 bytes, SHA-256 `91387415d7421b2490e4157220583eb787c043fa621ce0a619d7cdc4bbdad3c6`) from signed `ghcr.io/reidond/kedra-utm@sha256:b9a5c32e92b27ecc93e1a6f158befeeef3b446b8ca6c71754b9b04e7e7d46bef` (key `76ca7a65…66c6`, arm64 builder `ed3364f3…`, base `9f81b011…`); `SHA256SUMS` verified on macOS; no upload; diskless smoke not-run (no KVM on M2).
  - `create` registered `~/VMs/Kedra.utm` (UEFI + TPM with UTM's Microsoft-keyed secure vars, virtio-gpu-gl-pci, 96 GiB VirtIO disk). The owner installed through Anaconda with the disk encryption passphrase and administrator password generated into the owner's login Keychain. Booting again returned to the installer because the ISO stays first in the boot order until `detach-installer`; with UTM quit, `detach-installer --utm-quit` removed it.
  - Automation findings: this session cannot use `utmctl`/AppleScript (T3 Code lacks `NSAppleEventsUsageDescription`, so macOS returns -1743 without a prompt); UTM 5.0.6's `utm://` handler implements only `downloadVM`; the auto-mode safety classifier refused a synthetic-input helper, so the owner operated the window and the agent read screen captures.
  - Networking: with UTM Shared Network the guest received only IPv6 (ULA + link-local; DHCPv4 began and never leased; host `bootpd` not running; several VPN tunnels active), so `sysroot update enroll` failed closed with `lookup ghcr.io: i/o timeout`. Switching the VM to Emulated VLAN gave `10.0.2.15/24`. `kedra-utm.py create` now defaults to `--network emulated` and documents `--network shared`.
  - Progress: the helper now prints a status line before each registry verification and bootc staging and streams skopeo's copy progress and bootc output to the terminal (stderr); the JSON response channel is unchanged.
  - Layer reuse (owner requirement): `authenticate()` previously copied the whole signed image (6.3 GB) to a temporary `dir:` on every enroll/check/stage. It now copies into a persistent root-only OCI layout `/var/lib/sysroot/verified-oci` (`crates/sysroot-helper/management/oci_cache.rs`), still enforcing the signature policy on the registry manifest every time (`--remove-signatures` because the OCI layout cannot store signatures; the policy check precedes it). Existing blobs are reused (skopeo 1.22.3 / c/image v5.39.3 `oci_dest.go` TryReusingBlob); the manifest and config are rewritten from the registry on each copy and checked; refs outside booted/staged/rollback/journal/new digests are pruned before and after each copy; an inconsistent layout is discarded. Repeated `enroll` is refused before any transfer. The GHCR VM test now counts registry layer downloads, proves reuse, a discarded poisoned layout and rejection of a cached digest whose signature is withdrawn.
- Checks / evidence (owner screenshots, installed VM on UTM): pass — `mokutil --sb-state` `SecureBoot enabled`, `BootCurrent: 0005` first in `BootOrder`, SELinux Enforcing, LUKS2 `vda3` with btrfs `/var/home`; `sysroot doctor`: "Kedra utm — desktop session checks passed", all checks OK including `secure_boot` ("UEFI Secure Boot is enabled; kernel lockdown: integrity"), no failed system/user services, niri/Noctalia configuration, keyring, portal, audio, release trust, source `bfd42d14772c96a63fcf04a9eb31af67437b4230`; OpenGL: VirGL (`virgl`, OpenGL ES 3.0 / GL 2.1 compatibility, Mesa 26.2.3); Vulkan: `Virtio-GPU Venus (Apple M2 Pro)`, driver `venus`, API 1.4.334 (llvmpipe also listed; Mesa's dzn ICD skips itself with a harmless loader warning); `systemctl --failed` and `--user --failed` empty. Pass — local rust:1.98.1 arm64 fmt, Clippy `-D warnings`, release build, e2e 3/3, release-interop; local registry experiments of the cache (layer downloads 6 → 2 on repeat; 1 new layer for a second image; withdrawn signature rejected with no blob fetched). Fail then fixed — enroll over Shared Network (no IPv4). In-progress — enroll over Emulated VLAN with the old full-download helper. Not-run — Actions for this branch; clipboard/resize via spice-vdagent; GTK 4 rendering review; Bitwarden and Codex on aarch64 in the VM.

### WL-20260926-02 — 2026-09-26 — Post-install TPM2 disk unlock command
- Agent / state: Claude Code (Opus 5.5) with implementation and adversarial-review subagents; in-progress.
- Scope / base: `codex/tpm-unlock` from main `15c1a16`; all targets. Owner request: avoid typing the LUKS passphrase at every boot; decided post-install (Anaconda 44 has no TPM enrollment, `%post` cannot reuse the GUI passphrase, and install-time PCR 7 can differ from the installed boot).
- Completed: `sysroot setup tpm-unlock [--with-pin] [--replace] [--remove] [--dry-run]` (`crates/sysroot/setup.rs`) replaces the `setup` research gate. Unprivileged preflight: Secure Boot enforced (shared efivar reader), exactly one TPM 2.0 with `/dev/tpmrmN`, PCR 7 sha256 measured, exactly one LUKS2 volume under `/`, `/sysroot`, `/var` from `lsblk --json` with `/dev/disk/by-uuid` and `rd.luks.uuid` agreement, no key-file kernel options. Privileged steps run `/usr/bin/sudo -- /usr/bin/systemd-cryptenroll` with explicit arguments and the caller's terminal (sysroot never handles the passphrase), bind `--tpm2-pcrs=7:sha256` with empty `--tpm2-public-key=`/`--tpm2-pcrlock=` so no extra policy is added implicitly, require a passphrase slot to remain, verify slot lists before and after; `--remove` needs only the volume checks. The initramfs already carries systemd-cryptsetup, the tpm2-tss dracut module, the systemd-tpm2 token plugin and libtss2; systemd v259 tries enrolled tokens before prompting when no key file is set, so no crypttab/karg change. Docs: START-HERE, INSTALL, UTM README, kedra-security skill.
- Checks / evidence: pass — local rust:1.98.1 arm64 fmt, Clippy `-D warnings`, release build, e2e (`e2e_home_export` 2/2, `e2e_setup_tpm_unlock` 1/1, `e2e_source_targets` 1/1), release-interop; generated-LUKS2 + swtpm 0.10.2 experiment of the exact enroll/replace/remove arguments. Not-run — real boot unlock, fallback after a PCR 7 change, and all TPM paths on the owner's UTM VM; Actions for this branch.
- Remaining / blockers: qualify in UTM after the signed update carrying this command; without a PIN, console access yields the disk (GRUB/cmdline/initramfs unmeasured); on UTM the host holds `Data/tpmdata`.
- Next: push, qualify CI, merge, update the VM, run `sysroot setup tpm-unlock`, reboot.

### WL-20260926-03 — 2026-09-26 — Repository layout review (proposal only)
- Agent / state: Claude Code (Opus 5.5); completed, advisory only.
- Scope / base: `main` at `949abe5`, clean tree. Owner asked for a better project layout; no source, workflow, image or target changed.
- Completed: Reviewed tracked layout and path couplings. Contract-bound paths found: `.github/workflows/release.yml` is bound into signed image identity and checked by the installed helper (`crates/sysroot-core/image.rs`, `release.rs`, `build/release/material.py`); the payload roots `etc/`, `usr/`, `home/`, `packages/*.list`, `hosts/<target>/` are read from retained commits (`source::plan_revision`), recorded as `source_path` in signed `source.json` and validated in persisted home-review state (`sysroot-core/noctalia.rs`, `sysroot/home/text.rs`). Recipe paths are keys in resolved inputs (`build/release/refresh.py`), so moving one forces one changed publication per target. Proposed (not applied): root `Containerfile` into `build/`; one closed target table file embedded by Rust like `compatibility.json`, removing four `sys.path` imports of `material.py`; helper crate made bin-only with protocol in core and Linux primitives in a shared crate; CLI-only `noctalia.rs` moved out of core; `installer/media/` for the files copied into Anaconda; shared VM/test helpers out of `tests/vm/desktop/`; test directories named after their workflows; vendored rust-skills as a separate repository-local plugin (needs an AGENTS.md change). The owner rejected keeping the payload roots at the repository root. The owner then asked for the repository root itself to be a Linux filesystem. Current proposal: root `etc/` and `usr/` are the shared payload, with `etc/skel/` as the home baseline. All development material moves under `usr/src/kedra/`, which is excluded from the payload; target overlays go to `usr/src/kedra/image/targets/<target>/`. At the owner's direction, the root keeps `Cargo.toml`, `Cargo.lock`, `rust-toolchain.toml` and `rustfmt.toml`, with workspace members under `usr/src/kedra/crates/`. `worklog.md` also stays at the root, so Cargo, CI and the worklog location are unchanged. The proposal still conflicts with two AGENTS.md rules: "no `src/` at any depth" and the single skill tree. It needs a resolver that reads both layouts per commit and a comparison of home-review provenance by layer plus relative path instead of the raw `source_path` string, because no rebind command exists. The helper only hashes `source.json`.
- Checks / evidence: not-run — no build or workflow; inspection only (`git ls-files`, `git grep`).
- Remaining / blockers: owner decision on the directory names and which phases to adopt, and on the skills-contract change. Known consequences of the payload move: a pre-move CLI refuses home review against a post-move checkout until the machine updates; after a rollback to a pre-move deployment, home-review state that references moved paths is refused and preserved.
- Next: if approved, do the payload move as its own `codex/**` PR. Add a cross-layout e2e case and a legacy-A/moved-B home-transition fixture, then qualify all workflows.

The full candidate built in about 7 min, then every session fixture failed: `user@1000.service` "PAM failed: Authentication service cannot retrieve authentication info", with `unix_chkpwd: could not obtain user info`.
  - A stock Ubuntu 24.04 Docker 29.1.3 (systemd cgroup driver, overlayfs) on OrbStack's kernel starts the same user manager, so the cause is the runner host: Ubuntu 24.04's AppArmor `unix-chkpwd` profile attaches by path, including to the container's copy. The workflow now unloads that one profile and records AppArmor status and denials to confirm this.
  - fail — desktop `image_contents`: the x86_64 RPM names its entry `bitwarden.desktop`. The shared check is now architecture-neutral; the arm64 names moved to `utm-image.yaml`.
- Second run [36315527414](https://github.com/Reidond/kedra/actions/runs/36315527414) at `9a32ffc`:
  - Confirmed: `unix-chkpwd` was loaded before the step and absent after, and the session now starts. utm 11/13, desktop 10/12.
  - Remaining: the Bitwarden window and the GTK 3 app. The kernel audit log shows `profile="unprivileged_userns"` denials for `bitwarden-app` (sys_admin) and `bwrap` (setpcap, net_admin).
  - The workflow now also sets `kernel.apparmor_restrict_unprivileged_userns=0` on the runner.
- Third run [36316401867](https://github.com/Reidond/kedra/actions/runs/36316401867) at `35c0cfd`: the userns change fixed Bitwarden and GTK 3 startup. Desktop 11/12, utm 12/13.
  - fail — desktop `toolkit_file_choosers`: the GTK 4 portal chooser lost the leading "/" and searched for the rest. The test now waits for niri to report the chooser focused, then uses Ctrl+L plus the full path for GTK 4; GTK 3 keeps "/".
  - fail — utm `home_review_cycle`: `recovery.py` got exit 1 from `sysroot home recover <action>` after an interruption. It passed on desktop and in every local run. The probe now prints the journal phase before recovering and includes the CLI's stderr in failures, to tell a timing-dependent phase (published or validated) from a product defect.
- Fourth run [36317311862](https://github.com/Reidond/kedra/actions/runs/36317311862) at `e3be9c7`:
  - pass — utm 13/13, including `recovery.py` and both GTK choosers.
  - fail — desktop `home_review_cycle`: `sysroot home discard` refused with "an unmanaged Noctalia executable is running".
- Product race found (sysroot `home/activation/linux/native.rs` `writers()`):
  - Noctalia forks git for its plugin sources right after each start. Such a child keeps the name `noctalia` until its execve completes: first with Noctalia's executable (counted as "another writer"), then briefly with git's ("unmanaged executable"). A process exiting mid-scan also failed `read_link`.
  - Fix: skip processes whose parent is a Noctalia process, and skip processes that exited during the scan. A separately started Noctalia is still refused.
  - pass — Linux release build and `home_review_cycle` locally. Linux clippy is left to check.yml.
- Next: record the outcome of test-container.yml on both runners in a follow-up entry and STATUS.
